## Edge Cases & Boundaries

### POST `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/orders/customer/create` — Order Create — Missing Idempotency-Key (edge)

**Description:** Negative: order creation without the Idempotency-Key header must be rejected with 400. Idempotency keys are mandatory so network retries can never create duplicate orders.

**Result:** `PASS` | **Status:** `400` | **Time:** 15ms

**Request headers:**
```json
{
  "Accept": "application/json",
  "Content-Type": "application/json"
}
```

**Request body:**
```json
{
  "restaurantId": 360,
  "deliveryAddressId": 762,
  "specialInstructions": "Ring the bell",
  "contactlessDelivery": false,
  "paymentMethod": "CASH_ON_DELIVERY",
  "tipAmount": 20.0
}
```

**Response:**
```json
{
  "success": false,
  "message": "Missing required header: Idempotency-Key",
  "data": null,
  "timestamp": "2026-08-25T13:56:58.468858",
  "traceId": "31f84c090a13f559ceef99c39ecd9693",
  "spanId": "9eff826ef9aac4b8",
  "requestId": "16168415"
}
```

```json
}
```

```json
}
```

### POST `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/auth/login` — Login — Null Byte / Control Character (edge)

**Description:** Security: control characters in the email must be rejected cleanly (400) — never 500 or authentication bypass.

**Result:** `PASS` | **Status:** `400` | **Time:** 2ms

**Request headers:**
```json
{
  "Accept": "application/json",
  "Content-Type": "application/json"
}
```

**Request body:**

```json
{
  "email": "a\u0000b@bhukkad.test",
  "password": "Test@123456"
}
```

## Edge Cases & Boundaries

### POST `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/orders/customer/create` — Idempotency Replay Returns Same Order (edge)

**Description:** Placing an order twice with the same Idempotency-Key must replay the same order id (no duplicate order).

**Result:** `SKIP` | **Status:** `400` | **Time:** 0ms

*Skipped:* Order creation unavailable at probe time (first=400, second=400)

### GET `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/orders/customer/track/1523` — Order Track Rate Limit Enforces 429 (edge)

**Description:** Bursting the order-track endpoint must be throttled with 429 (never 500).

**Result:** `PASS` | **Status:** `429` | **Time:** 0ms

**Request headers:**
```json
{}
```

**Response:**
```json
statuses=[200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 200]... total=21 429_seen=True
```
